Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The organic_chemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 202. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 861 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the organic_chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^202 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 861-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
